# Daily video prompt pack — 09/11/2026 · Pig day (ថ្ងៃឆ្នាំកុរ)

Lucky signs: Pig, Rabbit, Goat · Careful: Snake
Day element: Yin Fire 🔥 (Ding-hai day; ធាតុភ្លើង) · every action scene today carries Fire magic: warm red-gold embers, small dancing flame-petals and rising sparks that never burn anything.
Format: vertical 9:16 (YouTube Shorts, Facebook Reels, TikTok), about 45 seconds: 4 Veo scenes of about 8 seconds, then a lucky-numbers scene of about 10–12 seconds.
Two versions: make the pictures once, then record a Khmer voice-over and an English voice-over (two videos, or one video with both subtitle tracks).
Meaning scenes: in the love, work, money and horoscope scenes the zodiac guardian and its animal act out what Chanda is saying, the guardian protecting and caring and the animal trusting (no real-life people), and every scene with Chanda on camera says which feeling she shows.
Ratings, lucky signs and lucky numbers come from the site's fixed rules and match the website's daily fortune for this date.
Voice-over wording: standard script (this pack was made ahead; the site's own daily wording appears the day before).

## Today's horoscope – all 12 signs
Each sign's prediction for 09/11/2026: general luck, love, work, money and advice, in English and Khmer, ready to copy into your Facebook, TikTok, Telegram and YouTube posts. The videos speak the same predictions: the 60-second video (the Pig's luck and love), the 180-second video (love, work and money), the long videos (every sign) and the full-fortune video. Lucky numbers are given only for today's lucky signs, as in the videos.
Wording: the website's standard lines for each sign's rating (this date's written fortune is published on the website the day before; open the Studio at mybirthsign.com/studio/ then to use it).

### Scene 8 – Goodbye – Chanda on camera (1:10–1:20)
Two-shot of Chanda and the pig together on the moonlit temple terrace, both clearly in frame. @Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, faces the camera, smiling and speaking to the viewer with natural lip movements. Hundreds of sky lanterns rise from behind the temple and form a great glowing circle around the full moon. ## 180-second video – YouTube
A 180-second video about the Pig, today's luckiest animal: 18 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add Chanda and the saved character @Pig as ingredients. Chanda opens the video on camera with the hook and narrates the rest off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice": it copies the style line, the scene and the voice together. Put the clips in order 1 to 18.

### Topic and audience
A three-minute guide to the Pig's Great Day: meaning, character, love, work, money and lucky details. For people born in the Year of the Pig and zodiac fans on YouTube Shorts.

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Daily fortune
   English: Born in the Year of the Pig? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរមែនទេ? នេះគឺពណ៌ ពេលវេលា និងលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ ព្រមទាំងការរំលឹកមួយផង។
2. Curiosity
   English: Out of all twelve zodiac signs, only one has a Great Day today. Is it yours?
   Khmer: ក្នុងចំណោមរាសីទាំង 12 មានតែមួយគត់ ដែលមានថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំងនៅថ្ងៃនេះ។ តើជារាសីរបស់អ្នកឬទេ?
3. Personality
   English: If you were born in the Year of the Pig, people may describe you as warm-hearted, generous, and easygoing. Today, that side of you shines.
   Khmer: បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរ មនុស្សជុំវិញខ្លួន ប្រហែលជាស្គាល់អ្នកថា កក់ក្តៅ សន្ដោស និងសុខដុម។ ថ្ងៃនេះ ចំណុចល្អនោះកំពុងភ្លឺ។
4. Challenge
   English: Can you guess today's luckiest zodiac animal? Three clues: warm-hearted, generous, and easygoing.
   Khmer: ទាយមើល៖ តើឆ្នាំណា មានសំណាងជាងគេថ្ងៃនេះ? តម្រុយ៖ កក់ក្តៅ សន្ដោស និងសុខដុម។

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Born in the Year of the Pig? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, Monday, 9 November 2026, is a Pig day. It is a Great Day for everyone born in the Year of the Pig. This is your day to shine!

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Generous and warm-hearted, the Pig symbolizes abundance, good fortune, and a happy home. Today, this good energy is with you.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
People born in the Year of the Pig are warm-hearted, generous, and easygoing. Pigs value comfort, kindness, and honest relationships.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Born in the Year of the Pig? Your luck is strong today. Stay happy, warm and generous. Say yes to good chances, and share your happiness with the people around you.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
Love and family today: A good day to reach out, share a meal and show your affection openly.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
Work today: Customers and coworkers respond well to you. A good day to present your ideas and close a deal.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Money today: A favourable day for steady income. Save a small part of today's earnings before treating yourself.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
Your lucky color today is brown. Wear something brown, or carry a small brown item with you, to keep good luck close.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
Your best time today is from 5 to 7 in the morning. It is a good time for important plans, meetings, or a fresh start.

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Your lucky direction today is the east. When you start something new, face the east, and walk forward with a calm and happy heart.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
For lucky numbers: 8, 24, and 288. Comment your lucky number below, and let's say it again: 8, 24, 288!

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
The Pig's lucky days of the week are Thursday and Sunday. Keep them in mind for plans that matter to you.

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
A gentle reminder: people born in the Year of the Pig can be overly trusting or indulgent. Today, stay balanced, and listen to your heart.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
Make a small wish today, and take one kind step toward it. A peaceful heart helps good luck find its way to you.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
Before the day ends, take a quiet moment to say thank you. Thank the people who help you, and thank yourself for trying your best.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: brown. Best time: from 5 to 7 in the morning. Lucky direction: east. Lucky numbers: 8, 24, and 288.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

11

12

15

16

18

19

20

21

22

32

33

## Long YouTube video – all 12 signs (about 5 minutes)
A long YouTube video (5:10): today's luckiest animal, an overview, then all 12 signs in order (two 10-second clips each), then a short closing. Lucky numbers are given only for today's lucky signs. Add Chanda and the 12 saved characters as ingredients (@Rat, @Ox … @Pig; each sign's clips name its own character). Chanda opens the video on camera and narrates the rest off screen; the animals never speak. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Put the clips in order 1 to 31.

## Story video – YouTube (about 5 minutes)
"The Golden Pig and the Moon Lantern" (ជ្រូកមាស និងចង្កៀងព្រះចន្ទ): an original fairy tale (5:00) starring today's luckiest animal. A new story every day: today the golden pig and Dara the little bird must bring the light back before the full-moon festival. Today's lucky color, direction, best time, lucky numbers and lucky days are part of the story. Make the picture of Dara once (prompt below) and add it, Chanda and the Pig picture as ingredients in Flow. Chanda opens the story on camera and narrates the rest off screen; Dara and the animals never speak (their words are told by Chanda). 30 clips of 10 seconds, in order 1 to 30.

### Character prompt – Dara the little bird (make once, keep it)
Create a photorealistic image of Dara, a tiny, lifelike magical blue-and-gold bird with bright, expressive eyes, soft round feathers and a little golden crest, as a small magical companion in a high-budget fantasy epic: realistic anatomy and textures with a soft warm magical glow, sitting on a glowing pink lotus by a still pond at night, a temple inspired by Angkor Wat and an enormous full moon behind, golden dust in the air. Cinematic lighting, beautiful depth of field, ultra-detailed. Square 1:1, full body visible, centered. No text, no letters, no watermark, no cartoon or plastic look.

## Voice-over – story video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Can one little golden pig bring the light back before the full moon rises? Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple in Cambodia, our story begins.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
But this morning, the moon lantern grew dim. Its light was almost gone. Without it, tonight's full-moon festival could not begin.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
In the middle of the pond, a golden lotus opened, and out came a little golden pig. Today was a Pig day, and the pig felt something special.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
A little bird named Dara flew down. "Little Pig," she said, "only you can bring the light back. Today is your lucky day!"

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Generous and warm-hearted, the Pig symbolizes abundance, good fortune, and a happy home. Dara smiled. That was exactly why the moon had chosen it.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
To relight the moon lantern, the little hero needed three lucky treasures: its lucky color, its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
"Let's go!" said the pig, and it set off on its adventure, with Dara right by its side.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
People born in the Year of the Pig are warm-hearted, generous, and easygoing. Pigs value comfort, kindness, and honest relationships.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
The first treasure was waiting in a garden of a thousand lanterns. Only one shone brightest. It was brown, the pig's lucky color today.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
The pig tied a brown ribbon around its neck. "Wear your lucky color," said Dara, "and good luck stays close to you."

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Next, they came to a crossroads with many paths. One path began to glow. It pointed to the east, the pig's lucky direction.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
But a wide river blocked the path, with only small stepping stones to cross it. The pig stopped. Sometimes, people born in the Year of the Pig can be overly trusting or indulgent.

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
Dara came close and smiled. "Breathe slowly," she said. "Trust your heart. You are stronger than you think."

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
Stone by stone, the pig hopped across the river. Each stone glowed gold under its feet. On the other side, it laughed with joy.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
The little hero learned that its worries were small, and its good heart was big. Balance and courage always help us grow.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
At last, they reached a great stone door in the temple. Carved above it were bells, stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
"The door opens only at your best time," said Dara. For the Pig today, that is from 5 to 7 in the morning. They waited patiently.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
When the time came, the pig rang the golden bells 8 times, its first lucky number. Ding! The door began to shine.

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
The door opened, and a stairway of light appeared. The pig climbed 24 glowing steps, its second lucky number, up to the top of the tower.

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
At the top of the tower, they found a shining pearl of moonlight. It was the heart of the moon lantern!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
Around the pearl, seven little lights twinkled, one for each day of the week. Thursday and Sunday shone brightest, the Pig's lucky days.

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
Time was running out. Holding the pearl carefully, the little hero hurried home, with Dara right beside it.

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting. The night was dark, and the full moon was rising. Was it too late?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
The pig placed the pearl inside the moon lantern. For a moment, nothing happened. Then, whoosh! A golden light burst into the sky!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
The lotus pond glowed again. Lanterns rose into the sky, and the full-moon festival began. Everyone cheered for the little golden hero!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
The full moon shone down warmly, as if to say thank you. The pig felt peaceful and proud. It had done its very best.

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
If you were born in the Year of the Pig, today is your day too. Like our hero, be brave, be kind, and follow your good heart.

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: brown. Best time: from 5 to 7 in the morning. Lucky direction: east. Lucky numbers: 8, 24, and 288.

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
And so, under the bright full moon, the little golden hero and Dara fell fast asleep, dreaming of tomorrow.

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរ ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។ ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
ហើយដូច្នេះ នៅក្រោមពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ វីរបុរសមាសតូច និងតារា ក៏លង់លក់ ដោយសុបិនអំពីថ្ងៃស្អែក។

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)

ប៉ុន្តែព្រឹកនេះ ចង្កៀងនៃព្រះចន្ទ ស្រអាប់ទៅវិញ។

6

បើគ្មានវា ពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់យប់នេះ

8

9

14

តារានិយាយថា "ជ្រូកតូចអើយ មានតែអ្នកទេ

15

ដែលអាចនាំពន្លឺត្រឡប់មកវិញ។

16

ថ្ងៃនេះ ជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់អ្នក!

17
00:00:39,118 --> 00:00:39,950
"

18

ជ្រូក មានចិត្តទូលាយ និងកក់ក្ដៅ។

19
00:00:42,199 --> 00:00:44,347
ឆ្នាំកុរតំណាងឲ្យភាពសម្បូរសប្បាយ

20
00:00:44,397 --> 00:00:46,333
សំណាងល្អ និងគ្រួសារដ៏រីករាយ។

21
00:00:46,383 --> 00:00:47,184
តារាញញឹម។

22

នេះហើយ ជាហេតុដែលព្រះចន្ទ បានជ្រើសរើសវា។

23
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:53,087
ដើម្បីបំភ្លឺចង្កៀងព្រះចន្ទឡើងវិញ

24

វីរបុរសតូច ត្រូវការរតនសម្បត្តិសំណាងបី៖

25

ពណ៌សំណាង ផ្លូវសំណាង និងលេខសំណាង។

26
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:02,547
ជ្រូកនិយាយថា "តោះទៅ!

27
00:01:02,597 --> 00:01:05,924
" ហើយវាក៏ចេញដំណើរផ្សងព្រេង

28

ដោយមានតារា នៅក្បែរខ្លួនជានិច្ច។

30

សន្ដោស និងសុខដុម។

31
00:01:14,324 --> 00:01:17,788
កុរឱ្យតម្លៃលើភាពផាសុកភាព សេចក្តីសប្បុរស

32
00:01:17,838 --> 00:01:19,950
និងទំនាក់ទំនងស្មោះត្រង់។

33

រតនសម្បត្តិទីមួយ កំពុងរង់ចាំ

34
00:01:22,240 --> 00:01:24,110
នៅក្នុងសួនចង្កៀងមួយពាន់។

35
00:01:24,160 --> 00:01:26,510
មានតែមួយប៉ុណ្ណោះ ដែលភ្លឺជាងគេ។

36

វាគឺពណ៌ត្នោត ដែលជាពណ៌សំណាងរបស់ជ្រូកថ្ងៃនេះ។

37
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:32,807
ជ្រូកចងខ្សែបូពណ៌ត្នោត ជុំវិញក។

38
00:01:32,857 --> 00:01:36,426
តារានិយាយថា "ស្លៀកពាក់ពណ៌សំណាងរបស់អ្នក

39
00:01:36,476 --> 00:01:38,807
ហើយសំណាងល្អ នឹងនៅជិតអ្នក។

40
00:01:38,857 --> 00:01:39,950
"

41
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:42,747
បន្ទាប់មក ពួកគេមកដល់ផ្លូវបំបែកមួយ

42

ដែលមានផ្លូវជាច្រើន។

43
00:01:44,407 --> 00:01:46,306
ផ្លូវមួយ ចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។

44
00:01:46,356 --> 00:01:49,950
វាចង្អុលទៅទិសខាងកើត ដែលជាទិសសំណាងរបស់ជ្រូក។

45
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:52,110
ប៉ុន្តែទន្លេធំមួយ បាំងផ្លូវ

46
00:01:52,160 --> 00:01:54,830
ហើយមានតែដុំថ្មតូចៗ សម្រាប់ឈានឆ្លង។

47
00:01:54,880 --> 00:01:55,790
ជ្រូកឈប់។

48
00:01:55,840 --> 00:01:58,110
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរ ជួនកាលជឿគេពេក

49
00:01:58,160 --> 00:01:59,950
ឬបណ្ដោយចិត្តខ្លួនឯងពេក។

50
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:01,715
តារាមកជិត ហើយញញឹម។

51
00:02:01,765 --> 00:02:04,264
តារានិយាយថា "ដកដង្ហើមយឺតៗ។

52
00:02:04,314 --> 00:02:06,028
ជឿលើចិត្តរបស់អ្នក។

53
00:02:06,078 --> 00:02:08,774
អ្នកខ្លាំងជាងអ្វីដែលអ្នកគិត។

54
00:02:08,824 --> 00:02:09,950
"

55
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:13,011
ម្ដងមួយដុំៗ ជ្រូកលោតឆ្លងទន្លេ។

56
00:02:13,061 --> 00:02:17,195
ដុំថ្មនីមួយៗ ភ្លឺពណ៌មាស នៅក្រោមជើងរបស់វា។

57
00:02:17,245 --> 00:02:19,950
នៅម្ខាងទៀត វាសើចយ៉ាងសប្បាយ។

58
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:23,371
វីរបុរសតូចបានរៀនថា ការព្រួយបារម្ភរបស់វា

59
00:02:23,421 --> 00:02:26,003
តូចទេ តែចិត្តល្អរបស់វា ធំណាស់។

60
00:02:26,053 --> 00:02:29,950
តុល្យភាព និងភាពក្លាហាន តែងជួយឲ្យយើងរីកចម្រើន។

61
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:32,950
ទីបំផុត ពួកគេមកដល់ទ្វារថ្មដ៏ធំមួយ

62
00:02:33,000 --> 00:02:34,314
នៅក្នុងប្រាសាទ។

63
00:02:34,364 --> 00:02:37,223
នៅខាងលើទ្វារ មានរូបចម្លាក់កណ្ដឹង

64
00:02:37,273 --> 00:02:39,950
ជណ្ដើរ និងប្រស្នាអំពីលេខសំណាង។

65
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,630
តារានិយាយថា "ទ្វារនេះ

66
00:02:41,680 --> 00:02:44,430
បើកតែនៅពេលល្អបំផុតរបស់អ្នកប៉ុណ្ណោះ។

67
00:02:44,480 --> 00:02:46,110
" សម្រាប់ជ្រូកថ្ងៃនេះ

68
00:02:46,160 --> 00:02:48,110
គឺម៉ោង 5 ដល់ម៉ោង 7 ព្រឹក។

69
00:02:48,160 --> 00:02:49,950
ពួកគេរង់ចាំដោយអត់ធ្មត់។

70
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:52,897
ពេលដល់ម៉ោង ជ្រូកវាយកណ្ដឹងមាស

71
00:02:52,947 --> 00:02:56,687
ចំនួន 8 ដង ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីមួយរបស់វា។

72
00:02:56,737 --> 00:02:57,950
ទីង!

73
00:02:58,000 --> 00:02:59,950
ទ្វារចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។

74
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:03,224
ទ្វារបើក ហើយមានជណ្ដើរពន្លឺមួយ លេចឡើង។

75
00:03:03,274 --> 00:03:05,968
ជ្រូកឡើងជណ្ដើរភ្លឺ ចំនួន 24 កាំ

76
00:03:06,018 --> 00:03:09,950
ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីពីររបស់វា រហូតដល់កំពូលប្រាង្គ។

77
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:11,768
នៅលើកំពូលប្រាង្គ

78
00:03:11,818 --> 00:03:16,541
ពួកគេបានរកឃើញគុជពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទ ភ្លឺចែងចាំង។

79
00:03:16,591 --> 00:03:19,950
វាគឺជាបេះដូង នៃចង្កៀងព្រះចន្ទ!

80
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:22,142
នៅជុំវិញគុជ មានពន្លឺតូចៗប្រាំពីរ

81
00:03:22,192 --> 00:03:23,169
ភ្លឺភ្លឹបភ្លែតៗ

82
00:03:23,219 --> 00:03:25,429
មួយសម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនីមួយៗក្នុងសប្ដាហ៍។

83
00:03:25,479 --> 00:03:27,416
ថ្ងៃព្រហស្បតិ៍ និងថ្ងៃអាទិត្យ

84
00:03:27,466 --> 00:03:29,950
ភ្លឺជាងគេ ដែលជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំកុរ។

85
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:31,703
ពេលវេលាជិតអស់ហើយ។

86
00:03:31,753 --> 00:03:35,311
វីរបុរសតូច កាន់គុជយ៉ាងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន

87
00:03:35,361 --> 00:03:39,950
ហើយប្រញាប់ត្រឡប់ទៅផ្ទះវិញ ដោយមានតារា នៅក្បែរ។

88
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:44,485
នៅឯស្រះវិញ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា កំពុងរង់ចាំ។

89
00:03:44,535 --> 00:03:48,438
យប់ងងឹត ហើយព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ កំពុងរះ។

90
00:03:48,488 --> 00:03:49,950
តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

91
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:53,315
ជ្រូកដាក់គុជចូលក្នុងចង្កៀងព្រះចន្ទ។

92
00:03:53,365 --> 00:03:56,008
មួយសន្ទុះ គ្មានអ្វីកើតឡើងទេ។

93
00:03:56,058 --> 00:03:57,354
បន្ទាប់មក វឺត!

94
00:03:57,404 --> 00:03:59,950
ពន្លឺមាសមួយ បាញ់ឡើងទៅលើមេឃ!

95
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:02,491
ស្រះផ្កាឈូក ភ្លឺចែងចាំងម្ដងទៀត។

96
00:04:02,541 --> 00:04:03,639
គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ

97
00:04:03,689 --> 00:04:06,835
ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។

98
00:04:06,885 --> 00:04:09,950
អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

99
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:13,095
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក

100
00:04:13,145 --> 00:04:14,950
ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។

101
00:04:15,000 --> 00:04:17,853
ជ្រូកមានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។

102
00:04:17,903 --> 00:04:19,950
វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

103
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,916
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរ ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។

104
00:04:23,966 --> 00:04:26,588
ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន

105
00:04:26,638 --> 00:04:29,950
មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

106
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,630
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។

107
00:04:31,680 --> 00:04:33,150
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ត្នោត។

108
00:04:33,200 --> 00:04:35,630
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 5 ដល់ម៉ោង 7 ព្រឹក។

109
00:04:35,680 --> 00:04:37,230
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងកើត។

110
00:04:37,280 --> 00:04:39,950
លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 8 លេខ 24 និងលេខ 288។

111

## Take-it-easy video – the unlucky sign, Snake (8 scenes, 80 seconds)
An 80-second video for the Snake, today's sign to take it easy (in the traditional day cycle the Snake clashes with the Pig day): 8 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add Chanda and the saved character @Snake as ingredients (the Snake is the only zodiac character in this video, from the first scene to the last). Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1 and 8; in the other scenes she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. It is one continuous little story in one place (the snake hurries, wobbles, stops to breathe, a little glowing koi fish keeps it company, it climbs the temple steps and looks out with hope), so make the clips in order and keep the same setting and light. No lucky numbers or colors in this video: it is about encouragement. Keep it gentle and realistic: a quiet day, not bad luck. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Put the clips in order 1 to 8.

### Scene 1 – The hook – Chanda on camera (0:00–0:10)

Below her, at the edge of the pond where a path of flat stepping stones leads up to the temple, @Snake (the Snake guardian with their snake) sits looking a little tired and lets out a quiet sigh with its mouth closed; it does not speak. ## Voice-over – take-it-easy video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Does everything feel a bit off today? If you were born in the Year of the Snake, here's why.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, 9 November, is a Pig day, opposite the Snake in the zodiac circle. Some days don't go to plan.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
And that's okay. Everyone has days like this. A quiet day is not a bad day. It is a day to breathe.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
Be careful today: you can be secretive or prone to overthinking. Pause before you react. You are doing better than you think.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Be gentle with the people you love, and with yourself. And lean on a friend you trust.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
At work and with money, take small, careful steps. Finish one thing, then the next. Be kind to yourself.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
Slow is still moving forward. Every quiet day makes you stronger, and brighter days are already on their way.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Rest well tonight. Tomorrow is a new day, and it could be yours. Follow MyBirthSign for your fortune every day!

សូមប្រយ័ត្នថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ជួនកាលសម្ងាត់ខ្លួន ឬគិតច្រើនពេក។ សូមឈប់បន្តិច មុននឹងឆ្លើយតប។ អ្នកកំពុងធ្វើបានល្អ ជាងអ្វីដែលអ្នកគិត។

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
សូមទន់ភ្លន់ចំពោះមនុស្សដែលអ្នកស្រឡាញ់ និងចំពោះខ្លួនឯង។ ហើយពឹងលើមិត្តដែលអ្នកទុកចិត្ត។

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
ក្នុងការងារ និងលុយកាក់ សូមបោះជំហានតូចៗ ដោយប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។ បញ្ចប់រឿងមួយ រួចសឹមធ្វើរឿងបន្ទាប់។ សូមមេត្តាខ្លួនឯង។

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
យឺត ក៏នៅតែជាការឈានទៅមុខដែរ។ រាល់ថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ ធ្វើឲ្យអ្នកកាន់តែរឹងមាំ ហើយថ្ងៃភ្លឺស្វាង កំពុងមកដល់។

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
យប់នេះ សូមសម្រាកឲ្យស្រួល។ ថ្ងៃស្អែក ជាថ្ងៃថ្មី ហើយអាចជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នក។ សូមតាមដាន MyBirthSign រៀងរាល់ថ្ងៃ!

## Long YouTube video – full fortune, 8:40 (mid-roll ads)
A full daily fortune for YouTube (8:40, 52 clips of 10 seconds), long enough for mid-roll ads. In order: the hook, the Great Day sign (Pig) in depth, the two Good Day signs (Rabbit and Goat), the sign to take it easy (Snake) with its little story, the other eight signs, then a recap and goodbye. Add Chanda and the 12 saved characters as ingredients in Flow (@Rat, @Ox … @Pig; each part names its own character). Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1, 4 and 52; in the others she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. Set the clip length to 10 seconds, make the clips in order 1 to 52, and paste the chapters into the YouTube description.

### YouTube chapters (paste into the description)
0:00 Introduction
0:40 Great Day: Pig
2:40 Good Day: Rabbit
3:30 Good Day: Goat
4:20 Take it easy: Snake
5:20 All the other signs
8:00 Recap and goodbye

### Opening hook – 5 options
1. Every sign in one line
   English: One sign has a Great Day today, two have a Good Day, and one should slow down. Which one are you?
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ មានមួយឆ្នាំថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង ពីរឆ្នាំថ្ងៃល្អ និងមួយឆ្នាំគួរធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗ។ តើអ្នកជាឆ្នាំណា?
2. Before a big decision
   English: Before you make any big decision today, watch this: your lucky color, your best time, and whether to slow down.
   Khmer: មុននឹងសម្រេចចិត្តរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ សូមមើលវីដេអូនេះ៖ ពណ៌សំណាង ពេលល្អ និងថាតើគួរធ្វើយឺតៗឬទេ។
3. Speak to the careful sign
   English: Born in the Year of the Snake? Watch this before you do anything big today. Everyone else, your luck is here too.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់មែនទេ? សូមមើលវីដេអូនេះ មុនធ្វើរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ។ អ្នកផ្សេងទៀត សំណាងរបស់អ្នកក៏នៅទីនេះដែរ។
4. Name the winner
   English: Today the Pig has its Great Day, and one sign should take it easy. Is it yours?
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ ឆ្នាំកុរមានថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង ហើយមានមួយឆ្នាំគួរប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។ តើជាឆ្នាំរបស់អ្នកឬទេ?
★ 5. Comment first
   English: Comment your sign now. By the end of this video, you'll know your lucky color, best time, and direction for today.
   Khmer: សូមសរសេររាសីរបស់អ្នកឥឡូវនេះ។ មុនវីដេអូនេះចប់ អ្នកនឹងដឹងពណ៌ ពេល និងទិសសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។
★ = used to open this video today (the hook changes from day to day, and each one gives every viewer a reason to stay to find their own sign). To use another hook, replace the voice line in scene 1.

### Mid-roll ads
YouTube lets you add ad breaks in the middle of videos that are 8 minutes or longer; this one is 8:40. Put the breaks at chapter starts so they don't cut a sentence. Chapter starts after 2:00: 2:40, 3:30, 4:20, 5:20, 8:00. Two or three breaks are enough; too many make viewers leave.

តើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំអ្វី? សូមប្រាប់ក្នុងមតិយោបល់ ហើយ subscribe ដើម្បីទទួលជោគជតារៀងរាល់ថ្ងៃ។
លេខសំណាង (1 ដល់ 5 ខ្ទង់) សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ គឺសម្រាប់ការកម្សាន្តប៉ុណ្ណោះ មិនមែនជាការទស្សន៍ទាយ ឬការណែនាំឱ្យភ្នាល់ទេ។

ការអានរាសីចិនតាមប្រពៃណី សម្រាប់ការកម្សាន្ត និងការឆ្លុះបញ្ចាំងខ្លួនប៉ុណ្ណោះ មិនមែនជាការព្យាករណ៍វិទ្យាសាស្ត្រទេ។

### Scene 1 – The hook – Chanda on camera (0:00–0:10)
@Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, stands on the moonlit temple terrace beside the lotus pond, facing the camera, smiling warmly and speaking directly to the viewer with natural lip movements and small, friendly hand gestures. Behind her, the twelve golden-armored zodiac guardians stand in a wide arc on the moonlit temple terrace: the pig shines brightest with a golden aura, and the snake rests calmly inside a soft bubble of golden light. The animals move gently and look around curiously, mouths closed. Medium shot, gentle push-in toward her face. Use the saved Flow character @Chanda exactly as she is: same face, hairstyle, outfit and jewelry in every scene. She speaks with heartfelt enthusiasm: a joyful smile, bright eyes and lively, natural hand gestures, as if she can't wait to tell the viewer something special. Natural, believable acting with real micro-expressions, never exaggerated or theatrical. Sky: a gentle shower of tiny golden meteors falls far away behind the temple, each one a thin line of light; it stays in the background and never covers a face.

### Scene 8 – The magical moment (1:10–1:20)
The pig trots around its guardian, who swings a glowing rake in a graceful martial-arts form, each sweep drawing golden harvest light across the ground, as today's Fire magic swirls around them: warm red-gold embers, small dancing flame-petals and rising sparks that never burn anything. Mist swirls away from it in a ring as it lands beside a carved stone lion on the causeway, proud and happy. Low-angle hero shot. The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English

Comment your sign now. By the end of this video, you'll know your lucky color, best time, and direction for today.

លេខសំណាង៖ ប្រាំបី ម្ភៃបួន និងពីររយប៉ែតសិបប្រាំបី។ សូមសរសេររាសីរបស់អ្នក ក្នុងមតិយោបល់ ហើយរក្សាទុកវីដេអូនេះ!

ថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំកុរ គឺថ្ងៃព្រហស្បតិ៍ និងថ្ងៃអាទិត្យ។ សូមទុកសម្រាប់ផែនការសំខាន់ៗ។ ថ្ងៃធាតុភ្លើង!

រំលឹកបន្តិច៖ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរ ជួនកាលជឿគេពេក ឬបណ្ដោយចិត្តខ្លួនឯងពេក។ សូមរក្សាតុល្យភាព។ ជម្រើសល្អៗតូចៗ នាំមកនូវថ្ងៃដ៏សំណាង។

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)

ថ្ងៃនេះ ថ្ងៃទីប្រាំបួន ខែវិច្ឆិកា ជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំកុរ ទល់មុខឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ក្នុងរង្វង់រាសី។ មានថ្ងៃខ្លះ អ្វីៗមិនដូចការគ្រោងទុកទេ។

Scene 51 (8:20–8:30)

Scene 52 (8:30–8:40)
សូម subscribe និងតាមដាន MyBirthSign រៀងរាល់ថ្ងៃ។ តើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំអ្វី? ប្រាប់ខ្ញុំក្នុងមតិយោបល់ផង! សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

1

2

00:00:04,220 --> 00:00:06,739

4
00:00:06,789 --> 00:00:09,950
best time, and direction for today.

5
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:11,795

6
00:00:11,845 --> 00:00:14,610

7
00:00:14,660 --> 00:00:16,843

8
00:00:16,893 --> 00:00:19,950

9

10
00:00:23,462 --> 00:00:26,392

11

Find your sign in the chapters below.

12

13

14
00:00:35,048 --> 00:00:38,236

15

16
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:42,693

17
00:00:42,743 --> 00:00:46,322
for everyone born in the Year of the Pig!

18
00:00:46,372 --> 00:00:49,950

19
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,105
Generous and warm-hearted

20

21

22
00:00:57,241 --> 00:00:59,950

23
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:02,060

24
00:01:02,110 --> 00:01:04,812

25
00:01:04,862 --> 00:01:08,574

26
00:01:08,624 --> 00:01:09,950

27

28

29

31

32

33

34

35

36

37
00:01:38,365 --> 00:01:39,950

38

39
00:01:42,339 --> 00:01:43,740

40

Save a small part of today's

41
00:01:46,048 --> 00:01:48,740

42

43

## Keep the voice the same in every scene
Flow makes the voice fresh for every clip, so the voice text in every scene is written to describe exactly the same voice: the same words, in the same order, at the start of the audio line, for every scene and every video. To keep it that way:
- Always use "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Do not retype, shorten or translate the voice text, and do not add other words about the voice.
- Make all clips of one video in the same Flow project, with the same model and settings for every clip.
- Use the same language for the whole video (all English or all Khmer).
- Listen to each clip as it is made. If one sounds different, make only that clip again with the same copied prompt.

## Khmer voice – how to get clear Khmer
Every Khmer voice line in this pack is written for speaking: short sentences, a ។ at each pause, and numbers written out in Khmer words (for example ហុកសិបបួន, not 64) so the voice does not read them in English. Subtitles and captions keep the digits.

### If you use Google Flow
Use "Copy + Khmer voice" for each scene; the voice description is already included. Flow's Khmer can be unclear on some clips. Listen to every clip, and remake any clip where words are wrong, cut off or English-sounding. If Khmer stays unclear, make the clips without voice ("Copy") and add a recorded Khmer voice-over instead.

### If you use a text-to-speech app
- Language: Cambodian Khmer (km-KH). Pick a real Khmer voice, for example Microsoft Azure's female voice "km-KH-SreymomNeural" (used by many TTS apps and sites). Do not use an English voice with Khmer text.
- Speed: 0.85× to 0.9×. Pitch: natural. Pause between sentences: normal.
- Paste the Khmer voice-over text from this pack exactly as it is (Khmer script, no romanization).
- Make one scene at a time if the app cuts off long text, then join the clips in your editor.
- Before using a new voice, test it with these two sentences and listen carefully:
  សួស្តីអ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា! សូមស្វាគមន៍មកកាន់ MyBirthSign.com។
  ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំខ្លា។ សូមជឿជាក់លើខ្លួនឯង ហើយកុំភ្លេចអត់ធ្មត់។
  Check that every word is clear Cambodian Khmer, sentence endings are complete, and speed and volume stay even.

### Record it yourself
A native speaker reading the Khmer voice-over slowly and clearly is always the most natural choice.

## Style (paste first in every scene)
Vertical 9:16. Photorealistic live-action fantasy film, the look of a high-budget fantasy epic that blends Khmer royal heritage with the grandeur of a Chinese historical fantasy drama. Real human faces with natural skin texture, the zodiac characters saved in Flow, each named after its animal (@Rat, @Ox, @Tiger, @Rabbit, @Dragon, @Snake, @Horse, @Goat, @Monkey, @Rooster, @Dog, @Pig): a guardian warrior in ornate armor themed on the animal, with a lifelike animal beside them (realistic anatomy, real fur, feathers or scales), always exactly as in the saved character picture, noble and powerful (never cute toys or cartoons). Setting: a majestic Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat with towering carved stone, an enormous luminous full moon, deep indigo starry sky, drifting mist, a still reflecting pool with floating pink lotus flowers and ornate golden lanterns. A living, magical sky in every shot: thin silver clouds drift slowly across the moon, stars twinkle, a soft halo pulses around the moon and sky lanterns rise gently in the distance. Action and magic in the style of a Chinese fantasy (xianxia) drama: graceful wire-fu flying leaps, slow-motion martial-arts moves, flowing robes and capes, glowing sword energy and swirling qi light, always without blood, injury or fighting between characters. Golden magic: shimmering gold particles, flowing ribbons of liquid-gold light and floating gold dust that never hide a face. Physically real lighting: cool silver-blue moonlight, warm golden rim light, soft lantern glow, volumetric light rays; colors deep navy, imperial gold, ruby red and lotus pink. Cinematic depth of field, smooth slow camera movement, high-end visual effects, 4K detail. The zodiac guardians and their animals never talk and never move their mouths; only Chanda, the presenter, speaks. No text, no letters, no subtitles, no watermark, no logos, no cartoon or plastic look, no low-quality CGI.

## Characters (already saved in Google Flow)
Your characters are saved in Flow as @Chanda and the 12 zodiac characters named after their animals: @Rat, @Ox, @Tiger, @Rabbit, @Dragon, @Snake, @Horse, @Goat, @Monkey, @Rooster, @Dog and @Pig. For each scene, add Chanda and the characters the prompt names as ingredients; the prompts below point to them with @ (for example @Snake), so keep the names in Flow exactly the same.

### Character reference image prompts
- **Chanda (ចន្ទា), the presenter (make once, use in every video):** Create a photorealistic portrait from a high-budget fantasy epic that blends Khmer royal heritage with a Chinese historical fantasy drama: @Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists. Realistic human proportions, natural facial expression with a gentle closed-lip smile, real silk that folds and catches the light, real gold jewelry with true metallic reflections. Graceful, upright posture, one hand held open toward the temple as if presenting it, the other resting at her waist. She stands barefoot on a carved sandstone temple terrace with an ornate stone balustrade, beside a reflecting pool with pink lotus flowers; behind her, a temple inspired by Angkor Wat glows under an enormous luminous full moon and a deep indigo starry sky, with golden lanterns and soft drifting gold dust. Cool silver-blue moonlight, warm golden rim light on her face and hair, volumetric mist, cinematic depth of field, sharp focus on her eyes, ultra-detailed. @Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, stands in front of the Angkor-style temple at night, facing the camera and speaking to the viewer with natural lip movements, then turns and gestures toward the pond. She speaks with heartfelt enthusiasm: a joyful smile, bright eyes and lively, natural hand gestures, as if she can't wait to tell the viewer something special. Natural, believable acting with real micro-expressions, never exaggerated or theatrical. Chanda is the saved Flow character @Chanda. Then: magical and powerful. @Pig (the Pig guardian with their pig) stands on its glowing lotus pedestal on the pond in front of the Angkor-style temple under a huge full moon. Its eyes light up, a golden aura bursts around it, and it trots around its guardian, who swings a glowing rake in a graceful martial-arts form, each sweep drawing golden harvest light across the ground, as today's Fire magic swirls around them: warm red-gold embers, small dancing flame-petals and rising sparks that never burn anything. The lotus pond ripples with golden light, and golden petals whirl up around it. Dynamic camera: start close on its face, then sweep up and follow it through the air. ## Scene 2 – Lucky signs
Magical and powerful. @Pig (the Pig guardian with their pig), @Rabbit (the Rabbit guardian with their rabbit) and @Goat (the Goat guardian with their goat) fly in from three directions on trails of golden light and land together on glowing lotus pedestals in front of the temple. Magical and powerful. A flock of glowing golden birds rises from the temple roof and circles the full moon, and the huge full moon glows over the pond. The camera rises up and pulls back for a wide, epic view, leaving open sky at the top of the frame for a title.

Today is a Pig day. Pig, Rabbit and Goat are the luckiest signs today. Snake should take it easy, slow down and avoid rushing big decisions.

Pig: brown · 05:00–07:00
Rabbit: pink · 13:00–15:00
Goat: red · 05:00–07:00
Snake: yellow · 01:00–03:00

Today's lucky numbers for the lucky signs (1 to 5 digits):
Pig  8 · 24 · 288 · 8272 · 55225
Rabbit  3 · 61 · 487 · 6921 · 36324
Goat  7 · 72 · 728 · 7467 · 27729

Lucky numbers are for fun only, not a prediction or betting advice.

All 12 signs: https://mybirthsign.com

Traditional Chinese zodiac reading for entertainment and self-reflection only, not a scientific prediction.

#ChineseZodiac #DailyHoroscope #YearOfThePig #LuckyNumbers #Shorts

## YouTube subtitles (captions files)
Two files are saved next to this one: `2026-11-09.srt` (Khmer) and `2026-11-09.en.srt` (English).
Upload in YouTube Studio → Subtitles → Add language → choose Khmer or English → Upload file → "With timing". You can add both to the same video.
The timings are estimates for a 45-second voice-over; after you record the voice, drag each line to match in YouTube's subtitle editor, or use "Auto-sync".
